Title: Erster Theil der Theosophischen Lectionen, betreffende die Schulen des einsamen Lebens
Creator: Members of the Ephrata brotherhood and sisterhood (anonymous testimonies read to the congregation by Conrad Beissel)
Publisher: Ephrata, Pa.
Publication Date: 1752
Rights: Public Domain (published 1752, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Language: deu
Source: Internet Archive item erstertheilderth00unkn (https://archive.org/details/erstertheilderth00unkn), the item's own erstertheilderth00unkn_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: John Carter Brown Library; host metadata date: 1752. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-16. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: 7f4552cc83090582ccced08fe740a5db41bbe94b9853bcbf26436bc920c82187.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. The John Carter Brown Library bookplate noise in the first thirty lines is left in place; the preface (Vorrede) begins at line 30 of the fetched file. Fraktur OCR with f/s and b/h misreads.
Content note: Spiritual testimonies of the Solitary of Ephrata, written as statements of their inner condition and read aloud by Beissel; the community's inner register in its own words. No PD English translation exists.
